# Commands · E · part 2

1,410 entries, emil-kowalski to error-trace. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **emil-kowalski**: Ask what Emil Kowalski has published on an interface, motion or craft question, with the article named ([HKTITAN/skills](https://github.com/HKTITAN/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/khe-brain-emil/commands/emil-kowalski.md) · ★47)
- **emissions-legality-gate**: A **hard gate** that must pass before any flash: block changes to emissions-critical tables and OBD monitors for on-road vehicles, and confirm the jurisdictional legality of the intended calibration. This is the workspace's ethical and legal backstop. ([DaxxSec/cognitropy-lab](https://github.com/DaxxSec/cognitropy-lab/blob/HEAD/powertrain-control-module-reprogramming/.claude/commands/emissions-legality-gate.md) · ★1)
- **emissions-model**: Run a project-level emissions estimate from per-link delay, queue, and idle time. CMEM is the default fast inner-loop; MOVES4 is the regulatory mode. ([DaxxSec/cognitropy-lab](https://github.com/DaxxSec/cognitropy-lab/blob/HEAD/traffic-engineering-signal-timing/.claude/commands/emissions-model.md) · ★1)
- **emit**: Generate a standalone, org-owned Claude Code plugin (the org's agent harness) from .forge.org.yaml. Reads the org interview, renders templates/org-plugin/, and validates that ZERO generator identity leaked into the output. ([ktoulgaridis/forge](https://github.com/ktoulgaridis/forge/blob/HEAD/commands/emit.md) · ★12)
- **emit**: Emit one of the four team documents — /mega-sdd:emit &lt;prd|fsd|sit|uat&gt; dispatches the matching doc-pack skill (flags pass through) — or render any mega-sdd md into one shareable offline HTML via /mega-sdd:emit html &lt;file|dir&gt; (triggers "render html", "html-kan", "bikin html dari", "share ke tim tanpa Claude") — or author a cited executive summary via /mega-sdd:emit summary (triggers "emit summary", "rangkuman eksekutif", "ringkasan project", "bahan presentasi"). No arg → list the four docs with current maturity from their doc-control stamps. ([FarhanRiuzaki/Mega-SDD](https://github.com/FarhanRiuzaki/Mega-SDD/blob/HEAD/plugins/mega-sdd/commands/emit.md) · ★1)
- **emit**: Regenerate SUBSTRATE.md from every repo's substrate.json manifest — exit 1 means a manifest problem that CI treats as red. ([Flux-Point-Studios/fluxpoint-plugins](https://github.com/Flux-Point-Studios/fluxpoint-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/substrate/commands/emit.md))
- **emit-guardrails**: Write the project's guardrails out in other agents' native formats — AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, Kiro steering, Cursor rules — from the same spec, so one brief binds every agent.
